```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/evisdrenova/code/entire/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/hook_bench_test.go",
  "content": "//go:build integration\n\npackage integration\n\nimport (\n\t\"bytes\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/exec\"\n\t\"testing\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/benchutil\"\n)\n\n// BenchmarkHookSessionStart measures the end-to-end latency of the\n// \"entire hooks claude-code session-start\" subprocess.\n//\n// Each sub-benchmark isolates a single scaling dimension that appears\n// in the session-start hot path (see hook_registry.go → lifecycle.go):\n//\n//   - Sessions:      store.List() called 2x, ReadDir + ReadFile + unmarshal + repo.Reference() per file\n//   - SessionsXRefs: sessions × refs interaction (repo.Reference scans packed-refs per session)\n//   - PackedRefs:    go-git PlainOpen + repo.Reference cost with many packed refs\n//   - GitObjects:    go-git PlainOpen cost with large .git/objects packfile\n//   - Subprocess:    isolates git rev-parse and binary spawn overhead\n//\n// Run all:\n//\n//\tgo test -tags=integration -bench=BenchmarkHookSessionStart -benchtime=3x -run='^$' -timeout=10m ./cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/...\n//\n// Run one dimension:\n//\n//\tgo test -tags=integration -bench=BenchmarkHookSessionStart/Subprocess -benchtime=5x -run='^$' ./cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/...\nfunc BenchmarkHookSessionStart(b *testing.B) {\n\tb.Run(\"Sessions\", benchSessions)\n\tb.Run(\"SessionsXRefs\", benchSessionsXRefs)\n\tb.Run(\"PackedRefs\", benchPackedRefs)\n\tb.Run(\"GitObjects\", benchGitObjects)\n\tb.Run(\"Subprocess\", benchSubprocessOverhead)\n}\n\n// benchSessions scales session state files in .git/entire-sessions/.\n// listAllSessionStates() is called twice: once in FindMostRecentSession (logging init),\n// once in CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints. Each call does\n// ReadDir + (ReadFile + JSON unmarshal + repo.Reference) per file.\nfunc benchSessions(b *testing.B) {\n\tfor _, n := range []int{0, 1, 5, 10, 25, 50, 100, 200} {\n\t\tb.Run(fmt.Sprintf(\"%d\", n), func(b *testing.B) {\n\t\t\trepo := benchutil.NewBenchRepo(b, benchutil.RepoOpts{\n\t\t\t\tFileCount:     10,\n\t\t\t\tFeatureBranch: \"feature/bench\",\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t\tfor range n {\n\t\t\t\trepo.CreateSessionState(b, benchutil.SessionOpts{\n\t\t\t\t\tStepCount:    3,\n\t\t\t\t\tFilesTouched: []string{\"src/file_000.go\", \"src/file_001.go\"},\n\t\t\t\t})\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\trunSessionStartHook(b, repo)\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n\n// benchSessionsXRefs tests the interaction between session count and ref count.\n// For each session, listAllSessionStates calls repo.Reference() which scans packed-refs.\n// Cost should be O(sessions × packed-refs-size).\nfunc benchSessionsXRefs(b *testing.B) {\n\ttype scenario struct {\n\t\tsessions int\n\t\trefs     int\n\t}\n\tscenarios := []scenario{\n\t\t{5, 10},\n\t\t{5, 500},\n\t\t{50, 10},\n\t\t{50, 500},\n\t\t{100, 500},\n\t}\n\tfor _, sc := range scenarios {\n\t\tb.Run(fmt.Sprintf(\"s%d_r%d\", sc.sessions, sc.refs), func(b *testing.B) {\n\t\t\trepo := benchutil.NewBenchRepo(b, benchutil.RepoOpts{\n\t\t\t\tFileCount:     10,\n\t\t\t\tFeatureBranch: \"feature/bench\",\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t\tfor range sc.sessions {\n\t\t\t\trepo.CreateSessionState(b, benchutil.SessionOpts{\n\t\t\t\t\tStepCount:    3,\n\t\t\t\t\tFilesTouched: []string{\"src/file_000.go\"},\n\t\t\t\t})\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\trepo.SeedBranches(b, \"feature/team-\", sc.refs)\n\t\t\trepo.PackRefs(b)\n\t\t\trunSessionStartHook(b, repo)\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n\n// benchPackedRefs scales the number of packed git refs (branches).\n// go-git PlainOpen reads packed-refs, and every repo.Reference() call\n// scans it. Session count held constant at 5.\nfunc benchPackedRefs(b *testing.B) {\n\tfor _, n := range []int{0, 50, 200, 500, 1000, 2000} {\n\t\tb.Run(fmt.Sprintf(\"%d\", n), func(b *testing.B) {\n\t\t\trepo := benchutil.NewBenchRepo(b, benchutil.RepoOpts{\n\t\t\t\tFileCount:     10,\n\t\t\t\tFeatureBranch: \"feature/bench\",\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t\tfor range 5 {\n\t\t\t\trepo.CreateSessionState(b, benchutil.SessionOpts{\n\t\t\t\t\tStepCount:    3,\n\t\t\t\t\tFilesTouched: []string{\"src/file_000.go\"},\n\t\t\t\t})\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif n > 0 {\n\t\t\t\trepo.SeedBranches(b, \"feature/team-\", n)\n\t\t\t\trepo.PackRefs(b)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\trunSessionStartHook(b, repo)\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n\n// benchGitObjects scales the .git/objects packfile size.\n// go-git PlainOpen parses pack indexes; large packs may slow it down.\n// This also affects repo.Head() and repo.Reference() indirectly.\nfunc benchGitObjects(b *testing.B) {\n\tfor _, n := range []int{0, 1000, 5000, 10000} {\n\t\tb.Run(fmt.Sprintf(\"%d\", n), func(b *testing.B) {\n\t\t\trepo := benchutil.NewBenchRepo(b, benchutil.RepoOpts{\n\t\t\t\tFileCount:     10,\n\t\t\t\tFeatureBranch: \"feature/bench\",\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t\tfor range 5 {\n\t\t\t\trepo.CreateSessionState(b, benchutil.SessionOpts{\n\t\t\t\t\tStepCount:    3,\n\t\t\t\t\tFilesTouched: []string{\"src/file_000.go\"},\n\t\t\t\t})\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif n > 0 {\n\t\t\t\trepo.SeedGitObjects(b, n)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\trunSessionStartHook(b, repo)\n\t\t})\n\t}\n}\n\n// benchSubprocessOverhead isolates the cost of subprocess spawns that happen\n// during session-start. The hook calls git rev-parse multiple times (some cached,\n// some not) plus spawns the entire binary itself. This benchmark measures each\n// component so we can see what fraction of the total is subprocess overhead.\nfunc benchSubprocessOverhead(b *testing.B) {\n\trepo := benchutil.NewBenchRepo(b, benchutil.RepoOpts{\n\t\tFileCount:     10,\n\t\tFeatureBranch: \"feature/bench\",\n\t})\n\n\t// 1. Bare git rev-parse round-trip\n\tb.Run(\"GitRevParse_1x\", func(b *testing.B) {\n\t\tb.ResetTimer()\n\t\tfor range b.N {\n\t\t\tstart := time.Now()\n\t\t\tcmd := exec.Command(\"git\", \"rev-parse\", \"--show-toplevel\")\n\t\t\tcmd.Dir = repo.Dir\n\t\t\tif output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tb.Fatalf(\"git rev-parse failed: %v\\n%s\", err, output)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tb.ReportMetric(float64(time.Since(start).Milliseconds()), \"ms/op\")\n\t\t}\n\t})\n\n\t// 2. Seven sequential git rev-parse calls (pre-optimization baseline)\n\tb.Run(\"GitRevParse_7x\", func(b *testing.B) {\n\t\tb.ResetTimer()\n\t\tfor range b.N {\n\t\t\tstart := time.Now()\n\t\t\tfor range 7 {\n\t\t\t\tcmd := exec.Command(\"git\", \"rev-parse\", \"--show-toplevel\")\n\t\t\t\tcmd.Dir = repo.Dir\n\t\t\t\tif output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tb.Fatalf(\"git rev-parse failed: %v\\n%s\", err, output)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tb.ReportMetric(float64(time.Since(start).Milliseconds()), \"ms/op\")\n\t\t}\n\t})\n\n\t// 3. Bare `entire` binary spawn (version command — minimal work, no git)\n\tb.Run(\"EntireBinary_version\", func(b *testing.B) {\n\t\tbinary := getTestBinary()\n\t\tb.ResetTimer()\n\t\tfor range b.N {\n\t\t\tstart := time.Now()\n\t\t\tcmd := exec.Command(binary, \"version\")\n\t\t\tcmd.Dir = repo.Dir\n\t\t\tif output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tb.Fatalf(\"entire version failed: %v\\n%s\", err, output)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tb.ReportMetric(float64(time.Since(start).Milliseconds()), \"ms/op\")\n\t\t}\n\t})\n\n\t// 4. Full session-start hook (for direct comparison)\n\tb.Run(\"FullHook\", func(b *testing.B) {\n\t\tfor range 5 {\n\t\t\trepo.CreateSessionState(b, benchutil.SessionOpts{\n\t\t\t\tStepCount:    3,\n\t\t\t\tFilesTouched: []string{\"src/file_000.go\"},\n\t\t\t})\n\t\t}\n\t\trunSessionStartHook(b, repo)\n\t})\n}\n\n// runSessionStartHook is the shared benchmark loop that invokes the session-start\n// hook as a subprocess and reports latency in ms/op.\nfunc runSessionStartHook(b *testing.B, repo *benchutil.BenchRepo) {\n\tb.Helper()\n\n\tstdinPayload, err := json.Marshal(map[string]string{\n\t\t\"session_id\":      \"bench-session\",\n\t\t\"transcript_path\": \"\",\n\t})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tb.Fatalf(\"marshal stdin: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tbinary := getTestBinary()\n\tclaudeProjectDir := b.TempDir()\n\n\tb.ResetTimer()\n\tfor range b.N {\n\t\tstart := time.Now()\n\n\t\tcmd := exec.Command(binary, \"hooks\", \"claude-code\", \"session-start\")\n\t\tcmd.Dir = repo.Dir\n\t\tcmd.Stdin = bytes.NewReader(stdinPayload)\n\t\tcmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),\n\t\t\t\"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"+claudeProjectDir,\n\t\t)\n\n\t\toutput, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tb.Fatalf(\"session-start hook failed: %v\\nOutput: %s\", err, output)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tb.ReportMetric(float64(time.Since(start).Milliseconds()), \"ms/op\")\n\t}\n}\n"
```
```json
}
```

> AGENT

Nice improvement. Here's the before/after comparison:

| Component | Before | After | Change |
|---|---|---|---|
| `git rev-parse` × 1 | 10ms | 10ms | — (baseline, unchanged) |
| `git rev-parse` × 7 | 61ms | 74ms | — (external baseline, unchanged) |
| `entire version` | 14ms | 15ms | — (binary spawn, unchanged) |
| **Full hook** | **91ms** | **59ms** | **-32ms (-35%)** |

The caching is working. Before, the full hook (91ms) was *more* than binary spawn + 7 rev-parse calls (14 + 61 = 75ms). Now the full hook (59ms) is *less than* 7 uncached rev-parse calls (74ms), meaning the caching eliminated most of the subprocess spawns inside the hook.

The remaining 59ms breaks down roughly as:
- ~15ms: `entire` binary spawn + Go runtime + cobra init
- ~10-20ms: 1-2 uncached `git rev-parse` calls (the first call that populates each cache)
- ~20-30ms: actual hook logic (session state I/O, ref lookups, settings load)

Want me to run the full suite across all dimensions too?

> AGENT

Here's the summary of the full benchmark suite results after your caching optimizations:

## Session-Start Hook Benchmark Results (Post-Optimization)

### Key Win: -35% latency on the full hook
The `git rev-parse` caching eliminated redundant subprocess spawns, dropping the full hook from **91ms → 59ms**.

### Results by Dimension

| Dimension | Range Tested | Latency Range | Trend |
|-----------|-------------|---------------|-------|
| **Sessions** | 0 → 200 files | 59–79ms | Slight scaling at 200 sessions |
| **SessionsXRefs** | 5–100 sessions × 10–500 refs | 65–77ms | Flat |
| **PackedRefs** | 0 → 2,000 refs | 59–72ms | Flat |
| **GitObjects** | 0 → 10,000 objects | 62–70ms | Flat |
| **Subprocess/FullHook** | baseline | **59ms** | — |

### Subprocess Breakdown (where the time goes now)

| Component | Latency | % of FullHook |
|-----------|---------|---------------|
| `git rev-parse` × 1 | ~9ms | ~15% |
| `git rev-parse` × 7 (pre-opt baseline) | ~61ms | was 67% |
| `entire version` (binary spawn) | ~14ms | ~24% |
| **Full hook (post-opt)** | **~59ms** | 100% |

### Takeaways

1. **Caching worked** — the redundant `git rev-parse` calls were the dominant cost and are now eliminated
2. **No scaling bottlenecks** — sessions, refs, and git objects all stay flat up to the tested limits
3. **Remaining ~60ms is mostly irreducible**: binary spawn (~15ms) + 1–2 cached git calls (~10ms) + hook logic + go-git PlainOpen (~25ms)
4. **Sessions at 200** show slight scaling (~79ms), but that's an extreme case — typical users have 1–5

```
   151→cleanup() {

   153→    git checkout "$current_branch" --quiet 2>/dev/null || true
   154→  fi
   155→  if [ "$has_changes" = true ]; then
   156→    git stash pop --quiet 2>/dev/null || true
   157→  fi
   158→  rm -rf "$tmpdir"
   159→}

   163→run_bench() {
   164→  local label="$1" out="$2"

   173→  fi

   207→[ "$has_changes" = true ] && git stash pop --quiet && has_changes=false
   208→
```

> AGENT

I think the reviewer's comment is reasonable. The `bench:compare` script is ~85 lines of bash — significantly more complex than the other inline tasks in `mise.toml` (`bench:cpu` and `bench:mem` are ~5 lines each). The existing `mise-tasks/` scripts are small, focused shell files. Moving `bench:compare` there would:

1. **Follow the existing pattern** — complex scripts go in `mise-tasks/`, simple one-liners stay inline
2. **Make it easier to maintain** — proper editor support (syntax highlighting, shellcheck) for a standalone script
3. **Keep `mise.toml` readable** — 85 lines of bash embedded in TOML is hard to scan

I'd also move `bench:cpu` and `bench:mem` while we're at it to keep all bench tasks together, but that's optional since they're short.

Want me to make the move?

```
    89→# Get staged Go files, preserving paths with spaces using null delimiters throughout
    90→if ! git diff --cached --name-only -z --diff-filter=ACM | grep -z '\\.go$' | grep -zq .; then
    91→  echo "No staged Go files to check"
    92→  exit 0
    93→fi
    94→echo "Checking staged files for duplication..."
    95→git diff --cached --name-only -z --diff-filter=ACM | grep -z '\\.go$' | xargs -0 golangci-lint run --enable-only dupl --new=false --max-issues-per-linter=0 --max-same-issues=0
    96→"""
    97→
    98→[tasks.bench]
    99→description = "Run all benchmarks"
   100→run = "go test -bench=. -benchmem -run='^$' -timeout=10m ./..."
   101→
   102→[tasks."bench:cpu"]
   103→description = "Run benchmarks with CPU profile (single package)"
   104→run = """
   105→#!/usr/bin/env bash
   106→set -euo pipefail
   107→PKG="${BENCH_PKG:-./cmd/entire/cli/benchutil/}"
   108→echo "Profiling package: $PKG (override with BENCH_PKG=./path/to/pkg)"
   109→go test -bench=. -benchmem -run='^$' -cpuprofile=cpu.prof -timeout=10m "$PKG"
   110→echo "Profile saved to cpu.prof. View with: go tool pprof -http=:8080 cpu.prof"
   111→"""
   112→
   113→[tasks."bench:mem"]
   114→description = "Run benchmarks with memory profile (single package)"
   115→run = """
   116→#!/usr/bin/env bash
   117→set -euo pipefail
   118→PKG="${BENCH_PKG:-./cmd/entire/cli/benchutil/}"
   119→echo "Profiling package: $PKG (override with BENCH_PKG=./path/to/pkg)"
   120→go test -bench=. -benchmem -run='^$' -memprofile=mem.prof -timeout=10m "$PKG"
   121→echo "Profile saved to mem.prof. View with: go tool pprof -http=:8080 mem.prof"
   122→"""
   123→
   124→[tasks."bench:compare"]
   125→description = "Compare benchmarks between current branch and base ref (requires benchstat: go install golang.org/x/perf/cmd/benchstat@latest)"
   126→run = """
   127→#!/usr/bin/env bash
   128→set -euo pipefail
   129→
   130→BENCH_PATTERN="${BENCH_PATTERN:-.}"
   131→BENCH_PKG="${BENCH_PKG:-./...}"
   132→BENCH_COUNT="${BENCH_COUNT:-6}"
   133→BENCH_TIMEOUT="${BENCH_TIMEOUT:-10m}"
   134→BASE_REF="${BASE_REF:-main}"
   135→
   136→if ! command -v benchstat &>/dev/null; then
   137→  echo "benchstat not found. Install with: go install golang.org/x/perf/cmd/benchstat@latest"
   138→  exit 1
   139→fi
   140→
   141→current_branch=$(git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD)
   142→if [ "$current_branch" = "$BASE_REF" ]; then
   143→  echo "Already on $BASE_REF — nothing to compare. Run from a feature branch."
   144→  exit 1
   145→fi
   146→
   147→tmpdir=$(mktemp -d)
   148→on_base_ref=false
   149→has_changes=false
   150→
   151→cleanup() {
   152→  if [ "$on_base_ref" = true ]; then
   153→    git checkout "$current_branch" --quiet 2>/dev/null || true
   154→  fi
   155→  if [ "$has_changes" = true ]; then
   156→    git stash pop --quiet 2>/dev/null || true
   157→  fi
   158→  rm -rf "$tmpdir"
   159→}
   160→trap cleanup EXIT
   161→
   162→# Filter go test output to only valid benchmark lines (excludes stderr noise like "✓ Created orphan branch")
   163→run_bench() {
   164→  local label="$1" out="$2"
   165→  echo "=== Benchmarking: $label ==="
   166→  go test -bench="$BENCH_PATTERN" -benchmem -run='^$' -count="$BENCH_COUNT" -timeout="$BENCH_TIMEOUT" "$BENCH_PKG" 2>/dev/null \
   167→    | grep -E '^(Benchmark.*[[:space:]]+[0-9]|goos:|goarch:|pkg:|cpu:)' > "$out"
   168→  local n
   169→  n=$(grep -c '^Benchmark' "$out" || true)
   170→  if [ "$n" -eq 0 ]; then
   171→    echo "  ERROR: no benchmark results captured."
   172→    return 1
   173→  fi
   174→  echo "  captured $n result lines"
   175→}
   176→
   177→# Use branch names as filenames so benchstat shows them as column headers
   178→new_out="$tmpdir/$current_branch"
   179→if ! run_bench "$current_branch" "$new_out"; then
   180→  exit 1
   181→fi
   182→
   183→# Stash, switch to base, run, switch back
   184→if ! git diff --quiet || ! git diff --cached --quiet; then
   185→  has_changes=true
   186→  git stash push -q -m "bench:compare auto-stash"
   187→fi
   188→
   189→old_out="$tmpdir/$BASE_REF"
   190→echo ""
   191→git checkout "$BASE_REF" --quiet 2>/dev/null
   192→on_base_ref=true
   193→
   194→if ! run_bench "$BASE_REF" "$old_out"; then
   195→  echo ""
   196→  echo "Benchmark does not exist on $BASE_REF. Showing current branch only:"
   197→  git checkout "$current_branch" --quiet 2>/dev/null
   198→  on_base_ref=false
   199→  [ "$has_changes" = true ] && git stash pop --quiet && has_changes=false
   200→  echo ""
   201→  (cd "$tmpdir" && benchstat "$(basename "$new_out")")
   202→  exit 0
   203→fi
   204→
   205→git checkout "$current_branch" --quiet 2>/dev/null
   206→on_base_ref=false
   207→[ "$has_changes" = true ] && git stash pop --quiet && has_changes=false
   208→
   209→echo ""
   210→(cd "$tmpdir" && benchstat "$(basename "$old_out")" "$(basename "$new_out")")
   211→"""
   212→
   213→[tasks."test:e2e"]
   214→description = "Run E2E tests with real agent calls (requires claude CLI)"
   215→# -count=1 disables test caching since E2E tests call real external agents
   216→run = "go test -tags=e2e -count=1 -timeout=30m -v ./cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/..."
   217→
   218→[tasks."test:e2e:claude"]
   219→description = "Run E2E tests with Claude Code (haiku model)"
   220→run = "E2E_AGENT=claude-code go test -tags=e2e -count=1 -timeout=30m -v ./cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/..."
   221→
   222→[tasks."test:e2e:gemini"]
   223→description = "Run E2E tests with Gemini CLI (sequential to avoid rate limits)"
   224→run = "E2E_AGENT=gemini go test -tags=e2e -count=1 -parallel 1 -timeout=30m -v ./cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/..."
   225→
```

```json
{
```

```json
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n#MISE description=\"Compare benchmarks between current branch and base ref (requires benchstat: go install golang.org/x/perf/cmd/benchstat@latest)\"\nset -euo pipefail\n\nBENCH_PATTERN=\"${BENCH_PATTERN:-.}\"\nBENCH_PKG=\"${BENCH_PKG:-./...}\"\nBENCH_COUNT=\"${BENCH_COUNT:-6}\"\nBENCH_TIMEOUT=\"${BENCH_TIMEOUT:-10m}\"\nBASE_REF=\"${BASE_REF:-main}\"\n\nif ! command -v benchstat &>/dev/null; then\n  echo \"benchstat not found. Install with: go install golang.org/x/perf/cmd/benchstat@latest\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n\ncurrent_branch=$(git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD)\nif [ \"$current_branch\" = \"$BASE_REF\" ]; then\n  echo \"Already on $BASE_REF — nothing to compare. Run from a feature branch.\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n\ntmpdir=$(mktemp -d)\non_base_ref=false\nhas_changes=false\n\ncleanup() {\n  if [ \"$on_base_ref\" = true ]; then\n    git checkout \"$current_branch\" --quiet 2>/dev/null || true\n  fi\n  if [ \"$has_changes\" = true ]; then\n    git stash pop --quiet 2>/dev/null || true\n  fi\n  rm -rf \"$tmpdir\"\n}\ntrap cleanup EXIT\n\n# Filter go test output to only valid benchmark lines (excludes stderr noise like \"✓ Created orphan branch\")\nrun_bench() {\n  local label=\"$1\" out=\"$2\"\n  echo \"=== Benchmarking: $label ===\"\n  go test -bench=\"$BENCH_PATTERN\" -benchmem -run='^$' -count=\"$BENCH_COUNT\" -timeout=\"$BENCH_TIMEOUT\" \"$BENCH_PKG\" 2>/dev/null \\\n    | grep -E '^(Benchmark.*[[:space:]]+[0-9]|goos:|goarch:|pkg:|cpu:)' > \"$out\"\n  local n\n  n=$(grep -c '^Benchmark' \"$out\" || true)\n  if [ \"$n\" -eq 0 ]; then\n    echo \"  ERROR: no benchmark results captured.\"\n    return 1\n  fi\n  echo \"  captured $n result lines\"\n}\n\n# Use branch names as filenames so benchstat shows them as column headers\nnew_out=\"$tmpdir/$current_branch\"\nif ! run_bench \"$current_branch\" \"$new_out\"; then\n  exit 1\nfi\n\n# Stash, switch to base, run, switch back\nif ! git diff --quiet || ! git diff --cached --quiet; then\n  has_changes=true\n  git stash push -q -m \"bench:compare auto-stash\"\nfi\n\nold_out=\"$tmpdir/$BASE_REF\"\necho \"\"\ngit checkout \"$BASE_REF\" --quiet 2>/dev/null\non_base_ref=true\n\nif ! run_bench \"$BASE_REF\" \"$old_out\"; then\n  echo \"\"\n  echo \"Benchmark does not exist on $BASE_REF. Showing current branch only:\"\n  git checkout \"$current_branch\" --quiet 2>/dev/null\n  on_base_ref=false\n  [ \"$has_changes\" = true ] && git stash pop --quiet && has_changes=false\n  echo \"\"\n  (cd \"$tmpdir\" && benchstat \"$(basename \"$new_out\")\")\n  exit 0\nfi\n\ngit checkout \"$current_branch\" --quiet 2>/dev/null\non_base_ref=false\n[ \"$has_changes\" = true ] && git stash pop --quiet && has_changes=false\n\necho \"\"\n(cd \"$tmpdir\" && benchstat \"$(basename \"$old_out\")\" \"$(basename \"$new_out\")\")\n"
}
```

```
    49→#!/usr/bin/env bash

   100→run = "go test -bench=. -benchmem -run='^$' -timeout=10m ./..."

   104→# -count=1 disables test caching since E2E tests call real external agents

   110→
   111→[tasks."test:e2e:gemini"]
   112→description = "Run E2E tests with Gemini CLI (sequential to avoid rate limits)"
   113→run = "E2E_AGENT=gemini go test -tags=e2e -count=1 -parallel 1 -timeout=30m -v ./cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/..."
```

tool_result
id: toolu_0185tsoTHkSTXAxukJbaSsPv
```
     1→#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2→#MISE description="Compare benchmarks between current branch and base ref (requires benchstat: go install golang.org/x/perf/cmd/benchstat@latest)"
     3→set -euo pipefail
     4→
     5→BENCH_PATTERN="${BENCH_PATTERN:-.}"
     6→BENCH_PKG="${BENCH_PKG:-./...}"
     7→BENCH_COUNT="${BENCH_COUNT:-6}"
     8→BENCH_TIMEOUT="${BENCH_TIMEOUT:-10m}"
     9→BASE_REF="${BASE_REF:-main}"
    10→
    11→if ! command -v benchstat &>/dev/null; then
    12→  echo "benchstat not found. Install with: go install golang.org/x/perf/cmd/benchstat@latest"
    13→  exit 1
    14→fi
    15→
    16→current_branch=$(git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD)
    17→if [ "$current_branch" = "$BASE_REF" ]; then
    18→  echo "Already on $BASE_REF — nothing to compare. Run from a feature branch."
    19→  exit 1
    20→fi
    21→
    22→tmpdir=$(mktemp -d)
    23→on_base_ref=false
    24→has_changes=false
    25→
    26→cleanup() {
    27→  if [ "$on_base_ref" = true ]; then
    28→    git checkout "$current_branch" --quiet 2>/dev/null || true
    29→  fi
    30→  if [ "$has_changes" = true ]; then
    31→    git stash pop --quiet 2>/dev/null || true
    32→  fi
    33→  rm -rf "$tmpdir"
    34→}
    35→trap cleanup EXIT
    36→
    37→# Filter go test output to only valid benchmark lines (excludes stderr noise like "✓ Created orphan branch")
    38→run_bench() {
    39→  local label="$1" out="$2"
    40→  echo "=== Benchmarking: $label ==="
    41→  go test -bench="$BENCH_PATTERN" -benchmem -run='^$' -count="$BENCH_COUNT" -timeout="$BENCH_TIMEOUT" "$BENCH_PKG" 2>/dev/null \
    42→    | grep -E '^(Benchmark.*[[:space:]]+[0-9]|goos:|goarch:|pkg:|cpu:)' > "$out"
    43→  local n
    44→  n=$(grep -c '^Benchmark' "$out" || true)
    45→  if [ "$n" -eq 0 ]; then
    46→    echo "  ERROR: no benchmark results captured."
    47→    return 1
    48→  fi
    49→  echo "  captured $n result lines"
    50→}
    51→
    52→# Use branch names as filenames so benchstat shows them as column headers
    53→new_out="$tmpdir/$current_branch"
    54→if ! run_bench "$current_branch" "$new_out"; then
    55→  exit 1
    56→fi
    57→
    58→# Stash, switch to base, run, switch back
    59→if ! git diff --quiet || ! git diff --cached --quiet; then
    60→  has_changes=true
    61→  git stash push -q -m "bench:compare auto-stash"
    62→fi
    63→
    64→old_out="$tmpdir/$BASE_REF"
    65→echo ""
    66→git checkout "$BASE_REF" --quiet 2>/dev/null
    67→on_base_ref=true
    68→
    69→if ! run_bench "$BASE_REF" "$old_out"; then
    70→  echo ""
    71→  echo "Benchmark does not exist on $BASE_REF. Showing current branch only:"
    72→  git checkout "$current_branch" --quiet 2>/dev/null
    73→  on_base_ref=false
    74→  [ "$has_changes" = true ] && git stash pop --quiet && has_changes=false
    75→  echo ""
    76→  (cd "$tmpdir" && benchstat "$(basename "$new_out")")
    77→  exit 0
    78→fi
    79→
    80→git checkout "$current_branch" --quiet 2>/dev/null
    81→on_base_ref=false
    82→[ "$has_changes" = true ] && git stash pop --quiet && has_changes=false
    83→
    84→echo ""
    85→(cd "$tmpdir" && benchstat "$(basename "$old_out")" "$(basename "$new_out")")
    86→
```
